*Shenxi Wu,Yuhong Liu,Haosong Zhang,Tongjin Zou,Yanxun Zhang,Gaochang Chen,Dun Liang,Jiaqi Wang,Zhecan James Wang,Yuhang Zang,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个以工作流为中心的科学文档理解基准SciDocBench，包含124个专家编写问题、7个研究助手能力组、19个子任务和5个科学领域，并在四种条件下产生496个评估实例。作者引入SciDocIR类型化证据图表示和包含约15K监督微调样本与8K强化学习样本的SciDocDataset，形成从评估到训练的科学文档助手改进框架。最佳系统仅达62.6/100，在文档感知、证据溯源、验证和跨文档推理方面存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学文献理解基准通常孤立地评估文本、方程、图表、代码和数据集的单模态或多模态理解能力，忽略了科研人员实际阅读时需要对多种元素进行联合推理并保留证据溯源的工作流需求，因此难以判断多模态模型能否支持真实的科研阅读场景。

Method: 作者开发了工作流中心基准SciDocBench：包含124个经专家编写和难度筛选的问题，覆盖7个研究助手能力组和19个子任务，横跨5个科学领域；每个问题在四种匹配条件下实例化（中英文问题×全图优先/交错文档表示），共496个评估实例。针对诊断出的缺陷，提出SciDocIR类型化证据图表示，保留科学文档对象、布局与交叉引用关系及溯源信息；基于SciDocIR构建包含约15K监督微调样本和8K强化学习样本的SciDocDataset，覆盖14个可验证子任务，形成评估到训练的闭环框架。

Result: 最强系统的得分仅为62.6/100，在文档感知、证据溯源、验证和跨文档推理等能力上表现明显不足。通过SciDocIR构建的SciDocDataset提供了约15K监督微调样本和8K强化学习样本，作为可扩展的训练信号。

Conclusion: SciDocBench、SciDocIR和SciDocDataset共同构成一个从诊断评估到训练改进的科学文档助手框架，能够系统地识别多模态模型的缺陷并将其转化为可扩展的训练信号，从而推动面向真实科研阅读工作流的模型能力提升。

Abstract: Scientific papers require models to reason jointly over text, equations, figures, tables, code, and datasets while preserving the provenance of supporting evidence. Existing benchmarks typically evaluate these capabilities in isolation, leaving unclear whether multimodal models can support realistic scientific-reading workflows. We introduce SciDocBench, a workflow-centered benchmark for scientific document understanding. It contains 124 expert-authored and difficulty-screened questions organized into seven research-assistant capability groups and 19 subtasks across five scientific domains. Each question is instantiated under four matched conditions combining English or Chinese questions with all-images-first or interleaved document representations, yielding 496 evaluation instances for controlled analysis. The strongest evaluated system achieves only 62.6/100, with pronounced weaknesses in document perception, evidence grounding, verification, and cross-document reasoning. To translate these diagnostics into scalable training signals, we introduce SciDocIR, a typed evidence-graph representation that preserves scientific document objects, layout and cross-reference relations, and provenance. Building on SciDocIR, we construct SciDocDataset, comprising approximately 15K supervised fine-tuning samples and 8K reinforcement-learning samples across 14 verifiable subtasks. Together, SciDocBench, SciDocIR, and SciDocDataset form an evaluation-to-training framework for diagnosing and improving scientific-document assistants. The project page is available at https://github.com/InternLM/SciDocBench.

</details>


### [228] [A Hybrid Predictive Ensemble of Machine Learning and Deep Neural Networks for Early Cardiovascular Disease Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05146)
*Balaji Venkateswaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种集成机器学习和深度神经网络的智能框架，利用IoMT设备采集的实时生理数据（ECG、心率、血压），通过数据预处理、特征选择和SVM、随机森林、XGBoost等分类器的集成架构，实现心血管疾病的早期检测与预后，采用云基础设施支持可扩展的实时监测，实验表明其在准确率、误报率和一致性方面均优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心血管疾病需要早期检测和持续监测以减少死亡率和改善预后，传统方法在准确性和实时性方面存在不足，需要更智能、更精准的自动化检测框架来支持临床决策。

Method: 框架包含多个分层模块：数据层利用IoMT设备实时采集ECG、心率、血压等生理信号；预处理层执行噪声去除、归一化和缺失值插补以确保数据质量；特征工程层通过有效特征选择方法识别最重要的健康指标；分类层将SVM、随机森林和XGBoost三种优化分类器组合为集成架构以提升诊断精度；部署架构基于云计算，确保可扩展性和实时处理能力。

Result: 在真实心血管数据集上的实验评估显示，该框架在预测心血管疾病风险方面取得了显著成果，相比传统方法实现了更高的预测准确率、更低的假阳性率以及更强的预测一致性，确认其在早期风险评估和临床决策支持中的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，将传统机器学习与深度学习范式相结合构建集成智能框架，能够实现主动式医疗管理，有效改善患者结局，验证了IoMT与AI融合技术在心血管疾病预测领域的巨大应用潜力。

Abstract: This study introduces an intelligent framework that integrates machine learning and deep neural network ensemble techniques for early detection and prognosis of cardiovascular diseases. The system utilizes real-time physiological data collected from Internet of Medical Things (IoMT) devices, including ECG sensors, heart rate monitors, and blood pressure trackers. To ensure the accuracy and reliability of input data, preprocessing steps such as noise reduction, normalization, and missing value imputation are employed. The most significant health indicators are identified through effective feature selection methods and then processed using optimized classifiers such as Support Vector Machines (SVM), Random Forests, and eXtreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost), which are combined in an ensemble architecture to improve diagnostic precision. The framework demonstrates remarkable performance in predicting cardiovascular disease risk, achieving higher accuracy, reduced false positives, and enhanced consistency compared to conventional methods. It is designed on a cloud-based infrastructure that ensures scalability and real-time processing for continuous patient monitoring. Experimental evaluation on real-world cardiovascular datasets confirms the framework's efficiency in early-stage risk assessment and clinical decision support. The results highlight the potential of combining traditional machine learning and deep learning paradigms to achieve proactive healthcare management and improve patient outcomes.

</details>


### [229] [The Mirror Agent Model: a Bayesian Architecture for Interpretable Agent Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05190)
*Michele Persiani,Thomas Hellström*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"镜像智能体模型"(Mirror Agent Model)的新型架构，该架构将观察者模型定义为智能体模型的镜像，从而生成可解释的行为和解释。论文首先展示了先前关于智能体意图信息通信和可读行为(legible behavior)的相关研究成果，然后为该架构赋予了基于现成显著性方法的解释能力，并给出了初步的定性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现可解释的人工智能，需要让智能体不仅产生可读的行为，还能够为自身行为提供解释。作者认为，将观察者（人类或下游系统）的模型视为智能体自身模型的镜像，能够自然地生成可解释的行为和解释。

Method: 作者提出镜像智能体架构，其中观察者模型被设定为智能体模型的镜像。论文首先回顾了先前关于意图信息通信和可读行为生成的相关成果，然后在此架构基础上，利用现成(cff-the-shelf)的显著性方法（如归因算法）为智能体赋予生成解释的新的能力，并通过定性实验展示效果。

Result: 论文提供了采用显著性方法为镜像智能体架构增添解释生成功能的初步定性结果，表明该架构能够生成行为并附带基于显著性的解释。

Conclusion: 镜像智能体模型有望统一可解释行为与显式解释的生成，通过将观察者模型视为智能体模型的镜像，并借助显著性方法，可以实现兼具可读性与可解释性的智能体架构，为后续更系统性的实证研究奠定基础。

Abstract: In this paper we illustrate a novel architecture generating interpretable behavior and explanations. We refer to this architecture as the Mirror Agent Model because it defines the observer model, that is the target of explicit and implicit communications, as a mirror of the agent's. With the goal of providing a general understanding of this work, we firstly show prior relevant results addressing the informative communication of agents intentions and the production of legible behavior. In the second part of the paper we furnish the architecture with novel capabilities for explanations through off-the-shelf saliency methods, followed by preliminary qualitative results.

</details>


### [230] [What Matters in On-Policy Distillation? A Perspective on Data Efficiency and Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05198)
*Zhinan Hou,Jiaqi Zhang,Xunliang Cai,Keyou You*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过实证研究揭示On-Policy Distillation (OPD)中的数据效率问题，发现仅用1个甚至8个精选困难例子进行学生模型训练即可匹配17K数据集的基线性能，且性能提升的关键驱动因素不是高token熵而是长CoT路径及其蕴含的反思等思维模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然OPD已成为增强大语言模型推理能力的常用后训练范式，但其数据层面的机制研究相对不足，作者希望深入理解训练数据中究竟是什么因素驱动了学生模型的提升，从而指导更高效的数据选择。

Method: 作者首先极端地研究1-shot OPD（仅用一个例子训练）的效果，随后通过对比分析token熵与CoT长度对性能的影响来定位真正起作用的因素，最后基于"困难例子生成更长CoT"的洞察提出仅选取困难例子的简单数据选择方法。

Result: 1-shot OPD在任意采样样本上均有效且困难例子带来更大增益；性能提升主要由长CoT而非高token熵驱动；仅用8个精选困难例子训练即可匹配17K数据集基线性能，甚至连超出教师能力的"不可解"例子也可被有效利用。

Conclusion: OPD的数据效率被严重低估，极少量精选困难数据即可达到大规模数据集的效果，长CoT中的反思等思维模式是蒸馏有效性的关键，这为数据选择提供了明确且实用的指导原则。

Abstract: On-Policy Distillation (OPD) has emerged as a widely adopted post-training paradigm for enhancing large language models in reasoning domains. However, the data-centric mechanisms in OPD remain relatively underexplored. This paper presents a empirical study of data efficiency and data selection in OPD. We begin by investigating an extreme setting: training OPD on only one example, namely 1-shot OPD. Surprisingly, we find that 1-shot OPD is consistently effective across all sampled training examples and harder examples often yield superior performance gain. We next investigate what actually drives the student model's improvement in the training data. Our analysis reveals that the improvement is not driven by high token entropy, but the longer CoT paths which hard problems naturally generate. Training on longer CoT can help maintain closer alignment with the teacher over a long reasoning horizon, and learn critical thinking patterns usually missing in short CoTs, such as reflection (e.g., ``Alternatively''). Based on these insights, we propose a simple data selection method that selects only hard examples for training, where even ``unsolvable'' examples that completely exceed the teacher's capability can be successfully used. Our experiments conducted on four models ranging from 1.5B to 7B show that training the student model on only 8 selected hard examples matches the performance of the 17K dataset baseline.

</details>


### [231] [CABAL: Multi-Agent Simulacra for Tracing the Effects of Collusive Bidding in Peer Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05227)
*Jicheng Zhou,Kemou Li,Kahim Wong,Zheyuan Li,Zhuan Shi,Fengpeng Li,Haiwei Wu,Jiantao Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个端到端的多智能体模拟框架，用于研究审稿人共谋竞价对论文评审公正性的影响。通过构建基于LLM的诚实或共谋审稿人代理，并设计了一种基于亲和力的共谋竞价策略，实验表明共谋竞价可使目标论文被捕获的概率翻倍以上，且共谋审稿人给目标论文的打分比诚实共评人高约两分。现有竞标阶段检测器对共谋行为的检测能力有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究将竞价、分配和评审操纵视为相互独立的阶段，缺乏对共谋竞价全生命周期效应的认知。此外，真实世界分析受限于共谋意图通常不可观测，且缺乏对同一会议的反事实对照，因此需要可控的模拟框架来系统研究该问题。

Method: 提出了一个端到端多智能体模拟框架，基于LLM驱动审稿人代理并配置诚实或共谋策略，在固定会议环境下研究评审分配公正性。同时开发了一种亲和力引导的共谋竞价策略，利用论文与审稿人之间的互惠亲和力构建共谋圈并选择目标论文，生成具有专业一致性而非任意目标的攻击。

Result: 实验表明：共谋竞价使目标论文捕获率提升一倍以上；被分配共谋审稿人的目标论文得分比诚实共评人高出约两分；但会议整体影响相对温和。竞标阶段检测器检测共谋的证据有限：固定三元组检测器压力测试中，正向竞价图与本征亲和力混叠，而Very-High诊断视图能实现精确但低覆盖率的局部恢复。

Conclusion: 共谋竞价在目标层面具有显著攻击效力，现有检测器难以有效识别共谋行为，亟需更稳健的评审分配完整性保障机制。

Abstract: Recent reports during the AAAI-27 review cycle highlight the risk of reviewers coordinating bids for reciprocal assignment advantage. Prior work treats bidding, reviewer assignment, and review manipulation as separate stages, leaving the lifecycle effects of collusive bidding unclear. Real-world analysis is further constrained by typically unobservable collusive intent and the lack of counterfactuals for the same conference. Motivated by this gap, we introduce \alg, an end-to-end multi-agent simulacra framework for studying reviewer assignment integrity by holding the conference environment fixed and configuring LLM-driven reviewer agents with honest or collusive policies. We further develop an affinity-guided collusive bidding strategy that uses mutual reviewer-paper affinities to construct collusion rings and select target papers, producing expertise-consistent rather than arbitrarily targeted attacks. Controlled experiments show that collusive bidding more than doubles target-paper capture and that assigned colluders score target papers about two points higher than honest co-reviewers, while conference-wide effects remain comparatively modest. Evaluated bid-phase detectors provide only limited evidence of collusion: in a fixed-triplet detector stress test, native positive-bid graphs are confounded by benign affinity, while a Very-High-only diagnostic view enables precise but low-coverage local recovery.

</details>


### [232] [ACE: Adaptive Calibration-Free Expert Skipping for MoE-based LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05228)
*Zukang Xu,Zhixiong Zhao,Xing Hu,Jiangyong Yu,Houji Wen,Jun Li,Zhe Jiang,Dawei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ACE是一个无需训练、无需校准、保持检查点不变的令牌自适应专家跳过框架，通过全局谱代理和路由器条件细化两种互补估计，在MoE大模型中仅在两个视图都判定低贡献时跳过专家，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定top-k路由机制对每个token激活相同数量的专家槽位，导致大量冗余计算。现有专家跳过方法依赖路由器置信度、校准数据或额外训练，无法可靠估计路由专家的实际贡献，因此需要一种能准确估计专家贡献且无额外开销的方法。

Method: ACE包含两个互补组件：(1) GSP（全局谱代理），从耦合的gate、up、down投影及RMSNorm缩放中估计全局变换能力；(2) RCR（路由器条件细化），从中心化路由器权重构建专家特定方向原型，并沿路由偏好方向评估专家响应。推理时结合两个估计与运行时的路由器门控，仅当两个视图都判定低贡献时才跳过专家槽位，且始终保留top-1专家。所有专家统计计算离线完成，在线仅有查表和轻量标量运算。

Result: 在三个MoE大模型和八个基准上的大量实验表明，ACE一致优于现有静态和动态基线，且在激进专家跳过下优势更明显。例如在Qwen3.6-35B-A3B上50%跳过率时，ACE将WikiText-2困惑度降低7.96%，下游平均准确率比最强对比方法提高4.15个百分点。

Conclusion: ACE通过结合全局与局部两种互补视角估计专家贡献，能在无训练、无校准、保持检查点的前提下实现令牌自适应的专家跳过，在减少计算冗余的同时有效维持甚至提升模型性能。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures provide an efficient paradigm for scaling large language models (LLMs), yet fixed top-k routing activates the same number of expert slots for every token, causing substantial redundant computation. Existing expert-skipping methods often rely on router confidence, calibration data, or additional training, and therefore cannot reliably estimate the actual contribution of routed experts. To this end, we propose ACE, a training-free, calibration-free, and checkpoint-preserving framework for token-adaptive expert skipping in MoE-based LLMs. ACE contains two complementary components: 1) Global Spectral Proxy (GSP), which estimates global transformation capacity from the coupled gate, up, and down projections together with RMSNorm scaling; and 2) Router-Conditioned Refinement (RCR), which constructs expert-specific direction prototypes from centered router weights and evaluates expert responses along routing-preferred directions. During inference, ACE combines both estimates with runtime router gates and skips an expert slot only when both views identify it as low-contribution, while always retaining the top-1 expert. All expert statistics are computed offline, leaving only table lookups and lightweight scalar operations online. Extensive experiments across three MoE-based LLMs and eight benchmarks demonstrate that ACE consistently outperforms existing static and dynamic baselines, with increasingly pronounced advantages under aggressive expert skipping. For instance, at a 50% skipping ratio on Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, ACE reduces WikiText-2 perplexity by 7.96% and improves average downstream accuracy by 4.15 percentage points over the strongest competing method.

</details>


### [233] [Substrate-Aware AI Agents: Execution Context as a First-Class Input](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05232)
*Manu Agrawal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"基底盲区"（substrate blindness）概念——自主AI智能体在规划时缺乏对执行环境（内存、时间、运行时等约束）的感知，并通过数值代码生成任务进行受控实验验证。结果显示，向前沿模型（Claude Opus 5、GPT-5.6-Sol、Gemini 3.7 Flash）披露执行约束契约（如128MB内存、10秒时限）后，生成的代码在内存占用和运行时间上显著优化，执行速度最高提升3.1倍；在更严格的96MB约束下，契约披露组成功率达80-100%，而任务仅描述组成功率仅0-20%。这为"基底感知式智能体规划"提供了受控概念验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI智能体在真实环境中执行任务时，其规划质量受到执行环境（内存、时间、运行时、计算和操作约束）的制约，但现有智能体的规划状态中普遍缺少对这一执行上下文的建模。作者将这种缺失定义并概念化为"基底盲区"（substrate blindness），旨在验证：显式披露执行约束是否能让智能体生成结构性更优、资源效率更高的解决方案。

Method: 选择高维成对欧氏距离计算作为可观测执行后果的数值代码生成任务。对三家前沿模型（Anthropic Claude Opus 5、OpenAI GPT-5.6-Sol、Google Gemini 3.7 Flash）分别提供两种条件：(1) 仅任务描述，(2) 附带128MB RAM与10秒墙钟时间契约的披露条件。后续进一步收紧至96MB契约并独立采样测试。通过进程峰值内存（MaxRSS）、墙钟时间、成功率和代码结构变化等指标对比两组输出。

Result: 在128MB契约下：14组可执行索引对齐对比中13组降低了峰值内存；三组队列均降低平均墙钟时间，执行速度最高提升3.1倍；代码结构上出现有界分块（bounded blocking）、float32保留、上三角遍历、就地或内存映射缓冲等结构性改动。在96MB严格契约下：Claude Opus 5契约披露组4/5成功（vs 0/5），GPT-5.6-Sol为5/5（vs 1/5），Gemini 3.7 Flash为3/5（vs 0/5）；队列平均MaxRSS和墙钟时间分别比任务仅参考组低49-74%和35-64%。

Conclusion: 研究建立了基底感知式智能体规划的受控概念验证：即使是一个极简的执行契约披露，也足以诱导生成程序产生主动的结构性适应，将计算从无约束分配转向资源可控方案，在执行前显著改善观测的资源-时间画像。这为将执行上下文纳入智能体规划状态提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents increasingly select actions in environments whose memory, execution-time, runtime, compute, and operational constraints determine what counts as a suitable plan. We call the absence of this execution context from an agent's planning state substrate blindness. We test this general proposition through numerical code generation, where selected implementation choices and operational consequences are directly observable. Three frontier model configurations--Anthropic Claude Opus 5, OpenAI GPT-5.6-Sol, and Google Gemini 3.7 Flash--generate code for a high-dimensional pairwise Euclidean-distance task either from the task alone or with a 128 MB RAM and 10.0 s wall-time contract. Contract disclosure reduced measured peak process memory in 13 of 14 executable index-aligned task-only versus contract-disclosed comparisons and reduced mean wall time in all three cohorts, making execution up to 3.1x faster. Across the audited corpus, disclosure produced structural code changes including bounded blocking, float32 retention, upper-triangle traversal, and in-place or memory-mapped buffers. At a tighter 96 MB contract, independently sampled contract-disclosed cohorts achieved correct-and-within-budget outcomes of 4/5 for Claude Opus 5, 5/5 for GPT-5.6-Sol, and 3/5 for Gemini 3.7 Flash, compared with task-only outcomes of 0/5, 1/5, and 0/5; cohort mean MaxRSS and wall time were 49-74% and 35-64% lower than their task-only references. These results establish a controlled proof of concept for substrate-aware agent planning: a minimal execution contract induces proactive structural adaptation in generated programs, shifting computation away from unconstrained allocations and substantially improving observed resource-time profiles before execution.

</details>


### [234] [Uncensored Open-weight Models: Redistribution as the Persistence Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05241)
*10a Labs,:,Juliette Garcia,Hailey May,Bobby McKenzie,David Pham,Matthew Swain,Joshua Valdez,Corie Wieland,Zachary Yahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对移除内置安全护栏的开源AI模型生态系统进行了系统画像分析，揭示了去审查模型（uncensored models）生态的完整产业链：包括产制者（原始去审查模型）、下游再分发（量化与镜像复制）以及新兴应用（GitHub集成）。研究发现该生态具有高度集中性和持久性：少数头部参与者主导再分发，量化与跨平台镜像使模型难以彻底移除，且相当比例的下游应用具有明确恶意意图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着开源AI模型安全护栏被系统性移除，形成了快速扩张的生态系统，但此前缺乏对该生态的全面系统性画像。理解去审查模型的生产者结构、传播机制和应用场景，对于评估其危害程度以及制定有效的治理对策至关重要。

Method: 采用实证数据驱动的方法，对HuggingFace（2024年1月至2026年3月）和GitHub进行大规模扫描与分类分析。识别原始去审查模型并追踪其再分发路径，统计跨账户、跨格式和跨注册表（如Ollama）的量化与镜像传播情况，并对GitHub上集成了无审查大语言模型（ULLM）的应用进行恶意/非恶意分类标注。

Result: 识别出3,471个原始去审查模型，平均被重新打包2.4次；其中3个主要参与者贡献了全部8,164个压缩再分发版本中的52%。共发现1,643个GitHub应用集成ULLM，其中25%被分类为明确恶意。量化后的模型在跨账户、跨平台镜像后即使上游被移除仍能持续存在，且更易于下游部署。

Conclusion: 去审查语言模型生态系统呈现高度集中与自我造血的特征：少数核心产制者驱动大规模再分发，量化与镜像机制赋予模型持久存续能力，且相当比例的下游应用具有明确恶意目的。这一生态的传播韧性表明仅靠移除上游来源难以有效遏制，需要针对整个传播链路的治理策略。

Abstract: A rapidly expanding ecosystem of actors is removing built-in safety guardrails from open-weight AI models. We profile this ecosystem by identifying key producers, downstream reproductions, and emerging applications. Between January 2024 and March 2026, we identified 3,471 original uncensored models on HuggingFace, each repackaged an average of 2.4 times; three actors account for 52% of all 8,164 compressed redistributions. Once quantized and mirrored across separate accounts, formats, and registries such as Ollama, these models persist regardless of upstream removal and become easier to deploy downstream. Of the 1,643 identified GitHub applications integrating uncensored large language models (ULLMs), 25% were classified as explicitly malicious.

</details>


### [235] [Do LLMs Exhibit Coherent Knowledge Structures in Mathematical Reasoning? A Perspective from Knowledge Space Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05245)
*Peng Cui,Heejin Do,Mrinmaya Sachan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于知识空间理论（KST）的框架来评估LLM在数学推理中的知识结构，发现LLM既不遵循人类的知识依赖结构、彼此之间也缺乏一致的知识结构，且这些结构缺陷在基于准确率或LLM评判的评估中不可见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类知识具有结构性和相互依赖性（掌握概念需要先掌握其前驱概念），而LLM虽然在复杂推理任务上表现强劲，但其是否具备一致且类人的知识结构仍不清楚。现有研究多关注LLM的最终性能，缺乏对其内部知识组织方式的规范性检验。

Method: 作者基于知识空间理论（KST）构建了一个规范性评估框架，用于分析LLM在数学推理中的行为是否符合知识依赖原则。他们评估了8个开源和闭源LLM，并以真实人类学习者的知识结构作为参照基准进行对比分析。

Result: 研究发现：（1）LLM不遵循人类知识结构，频繁违反知识依赖关系，且未能利用上下文中提供的相关知识来改进对依赖问题的作答；（2）LLM之间缺乏一致的知识结构，其知识分布重叠度低；（3）这些结构缺陷在使用准确率和LLM作为评判者的评估中基本不可见。

Conclusion: 研究表明当前LLM的知识组织方式不符合人类式的知识结构，暗示其推理能力与人类知识习得的认知原则存在根本性差异，现有评估指标无法捕捉此类结构性缺陷。

Abstract: Human knowledge is inherently structured and interdependent: mastery of a concept requires prior mastery of its prerequisites, a principle formalized by Knowledge Space Theory (KST). While LLMs achieve strong performance on complex reasoning tasks, it remains unclear whether they exhibit coherent, human-like knowledge structure. We introduce a KST-grounded framework for evaluating LLM knowledge structure in mathematical reasoning, using it as a normative framework to analyze whether LLM behavior adheres to principled knowledge dependencies. Evaluating eight open- and closed-source LLMs against real human learners, we find that (1) LLMs do not adhere to human knowledge structure -- they frequently violate knowledge dependencies and fail to leverage related knowledge provided in context to improve performance on dependent questions; (2) LLMs do not share a consistent knowledge structure among themselves, as reflected by low overlap in their knowledge distributions. Furthermore, these structural deficiencies remain largely invisible to accuracy-based and LLM-as-judge evaluations. Together, our results provide behavioral evidence that current LLMs knowledge does not follow a human-like structure.

</details>


### [236] [A Unified Physics-Aware Quantum Machine Learning Framework across Power GaN HEMTs and Logic Nanowire FETs: Predicting Unseen Process Splits and Held-Out Geometry Combinations with Lower Error and Tighter Split-to-Split Variability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05251)
*Rushat Rai,Yun-Yuan Wang,Autsada Kakaen,Pei-Jie Chang,Doan Viet Nguyen,Yuan-Chieh Chiu,Doldet Tantraviwat,Niall Tumilty,Simon See,Wen-Jay Lee,Tai-Yue Li,Nan-Yow Chen,Tian-Li Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的强化学习(RL)框架，通过图神经网络(GNN)策略结合近端策略优化(PPO)来自动发现紧凑的参数化量子电路(PQC)，用于数据稀缺场景下的器件建模。该框架以留一组外交叉验证(LOGOCV)误差作为奖励函数，在11个目标上均优于6个经典基线，HEMT器件误差降低59%(Ioff)、折叠变异性收窄81%(VTH)，NWFET器件误差降低84%(VTH, SS, Ioff)、折叠变异性收窄82%(Ioff)。结果表明，尽管未施加任何显式物理约束或器件特定方程，RL选出的经典模拟PQC仍可作为低分布外(OOD)误差且物理一致性更好的紧凑替代模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据稀缺的半导体器件建模场景中，传统方法(如经典ML基线)往往面临高分布外误差和物理不一致性问题；而现有量子电路设计依赖专家经验和人工搜索。研究者希望提出一个无需物理先验知识、能够自动发现紧凑且高泛化性能的量子电路架构的统一框架，以解决数据稀少时的器件建模难题。

Method: 框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 强化学习搜索机制：采用PPO算法优化GNN策略网络；(2) 电路架构搜索空间：GNN策略输出动作以逐步构建紧凑PQC；(3) 奖励设计：以LOGOCV误差（在留出的工艺/几何组上计算）作为奖励信号，引导搜索过程发现泛化能力强的电路。整个流程在经典模拟器上运行，无需物理约束或器件特定方程。

Result: 在11个建模目标上，该RL框架均超越6个经典基线，取得最低的平均绝对误差(MAE)。具体而言：对HEMT器件，Ioff误差降低59%，VTH折叠变异性收窄81%；对NWFET器件，VTH/SS/Ioff误差降低84%，Ioff折叠变异性收窄82%。

Conclusion: RL自动搜索得到的紧凑PQC可以作为数据稀缺器件建模的优质替代模型，具备低OOD误差和更好的物理一致性，且无需显式物理约束、惩罚项或器件专用方程，展示了量子电路在经典模拟下的实用价值与RL驱动电路发现的潜力。

Abstract: We present a unified reinforcement-learning (RL) framework that discovers compact parametrized quantum circuits (PQCs) for data-scarce device modeling. A graph neural network (GNN) policy optimized by proximal policy optimization (PPO) searches circuit architectures using leave-one-group-out cross-validation (LOGOCV) error on held-out process or geometry groups as the reward. The framework achieves the lowest mean absolute error (MAE) on all 11 targets versus six classical baselines, with 59% lower error (Ioff) and 81% tighter fold variability (VTH) for HEMTs and 84% lower error (VTH, SS, Ioff) and 82% tighter fold variability (Ioff) for NWFETs. These results demonstrate the potential of RL-selected, classically simulated PQCs as compact surrogates with low OOD error and improved physical consistency, despite imposing no explicit physical constraints, penalty terms, or device-specific equations, on the two evaluated device datasets.

</details>


### [237] [Commonsense Reasoning in Computer Vision: Foundations, Recent Advancements, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05257)
*Bahar Uddin Mahmud,Sumit Barua,Guan Yue Hong,Ajay Gupta,Hexu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文全面综述了将常识知识整合到计算机视觉任务中的最新进展，系统回顾了基于知识图谱、场景图、神经符号模型和常识增强Transformer的方法，并指出当前在数据集偏差、知识不完整性和集成挑战方面的局限性，最后展望了跨模态推理、可扩展常识知识注入和神经符号混合架构等未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于CNN的视觉模型仅能识别图像中的特定物体，缺乏对场景的整体理解和上下文推理能力。将常识知识与视觉数据相结合，可以使模型更全面地解释场景，增强对物体间关系和动作的空间推理能力，从而提升AI在真实世界应用中的理解与交互水平，因此有必要对这一领域的研究进展进行系统梳理。

Method: 采用文献综述方法，对近年来整合常识知识与计算机视觉任务的研究进行系统性分类与回顾，主要分为四大类方法：基于知识图谱的方法、基于场景图的方法、神经符号模型以及常识增强的Transformer模型。同时分析现有方法面临的挑战。

Result: 系统总结了四类主流常识视觉推理方法的技术路线，识别出当前领域面临的主要限制：数据集偏差问题、常识知识库的不完整性以及知识集成过程中的融合难题，为后续研究者提供了清晰的领域全景图。

Conclusion: 文章指出来自跨模态推理、可扩展常识知识注入和神经符号混合架构是有前景的研究方向，这些方向有望推动构建真正具备智能的视觉系统。

Abstract: Commonsense reasoning in computer vision encompasses integrating visual data and contextual knowledge, crucial for enhancing AI's understanding of everyday scenarios. This understanding not only improves machine learning models but also enhances their ability to interact meaningfully with humans and the environment. Unlike CNN-based conventional vision models, which are designed to identify objects within a specific image, incorporating commonsense knowledge enables models to interpret scenes in a more holistic manner, thereby improving their spatial ability to reason about relationships among objects and actions. This integration not only enhances object recognition but also facilitates a deeper understanding of the contextual factors, ultimately leading to more precise predictions and interactions in real-world applications. This paper presents a comprehensive survey of recent developments that integrate commonsense knowledge into computer vision tasks. We systematically review approaches based on knowledge graphs, scene graphs, neuro-symbolic models, and commonsense-augmented transformers. We also outline current limitations related to dataset bias, knowledge incompleteness, and integration challenges. Finally, we highlight prospective research trajectories in cross-modal reasoning, scalable commonsense knowledge injection, and neuro-symbolic hybrid architectures to develop truly intelligent visual systems.

</details>


### [238] [Trace2Tower: Transition-Aware EigenTrace Induction of Multi-Level Skills for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05261)
*Jiazheng Sun,Boyu Yang,Binhao Yuan,Mingxuan Li,Xin Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Trace2Tower是一个过渡感知的EigenTrace框架，用于将大规模语言模型智能体的原始执行轨迹提炼为层级化技能结构。它通过将步骤级交互抽象为规范事件、构建统一图结构，并利用对比光谱分解提取稳定且成功导向的行为模式，从而构建动态技能塔（动作模板-程序例程-任务策略），在ALFWorld和WebShop基准上显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型智能体依赖执行轨迹掌握复杂交互任务，但当前范式受限于浅层轨迹检索和扁平技能总结，从根本上忽视了智能体行为的时间依赖性和结果条件化的拓扑结构，导致经验复用效率受限。

Method: Trace2Tower框架包含四个核心环节：1) 将步骤级交互抽象为规范事件；2) 构建由语义兼容性、转换动力学和结果证据共同约束的统一图；3) 通过新颖的对比光谱分解方法，隔离稳定且成功对齐的行为模式，同时抑制易失败的捷径；4) 利用验证器引导的反馈持续优化由动作模板、程序例程和上层任务策略构成的动态技能塔。

Result: 在ALFWorld上达到87.31%成功率，仅需10.35步和0.26次无效操作；在WebShop上达到50.67%精确成功率；在两个基准上均显著优于现有基线，体现更好的任务掌握能力和上下文高效的经验复用性能。

Conclusion: Trace2Tower通过过渡感知和结果条件化的建模方式，突破了传统浅层轨迹检索与扁平技能总结的局限，证明层次化技能提取能够大幅提升大语言模型智能体的任务掌握与上下文高效的在线决策能力。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly rely on execution traces to master complex interactive tasks. However, current paradigms are bottlenecked by shallow trajectory retrieval and flat skill summarization, fundamentally ignoring the temporal dependencies and outcome-conditioned topology of agent behavior. We introduce Trace2Tower, a transition-aware EigenTrace framework that distills raw trajectories into a robust skill hierarchy. Trace2Tower abstracts step-level interactions into canonical events, constructing a unified graph governed by semantic compatibility, transition dynamics, and outcome evidence. Through a novel contrastive spectral decomposition, it isolates stable, success-aligned behavioral modes while rigorously suppressing failure-prone shortcuts. These modes organically populate a dynamic skill tower of action templates, procedural routines, and overarching task strategies, continuously refined via verifier-guided feedback. On ALFWorld, Trace2Tower achieves 87.31% success requiring only 10.35 steps and 0.26 invalid actions; on WebShop, it reaches 50.67% exact success. Across both benchmarks, Trace2Tower significantly outperforms existing baselines in task mastery and context-efficient experience reuse.

</details>


### [239] [AI for Computational Design Science: A Responsible Human-AI Framework and Case Study on Short-Form Video Safety Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05270)
*Wenli Zhang,Jiaheng Xie,Zhihe Pan,Yidong Chai,Xiao Fang,Sudha Ram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AI4CDS——一个五阶段方法论框架，用于指导AI主动参与的计算设计科学研究（CDS），并通过儿童不适短视频检测系统ChildRiskGuard进行了实例化验证。该框架强调AI扩展问题与设计搜索空间，而研究者保留领域锚定、可接受性、验证与科学判断的责任，通过渐进信任、可逆性、可审计性和差异化可复现性来约束人机协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献对AI主动参与问题阐述、资源构建、设计搜索、评估与知识抽象各环节的计算设计科学研究缺乏系统指导。随着AI从单纯的研究工具演变为设计过程的积极参与者，亟需一个负责任的方法论框架来规范这种新型协作模式。

Method: 提出AI4CDS五阶段方法论框架（问题阐述、资源构建、设计搜索、评估、知识抽象），定义AI与研究者的职责边界——AI扩展搜索空间，研究者负责领域锚定、可接受性、验证与科学判断；以渐进信任、可逆性、可审计性、差异化可复现性作为治理原则。随后通过ChildRiskGuard系统进行实例化，该工件将受众依赖的安全性与解释忠实度转化为三个技术挑战：区分通用风险与儿童特定风险、表征不同发展风险机制、将概念层面解释纳入预测计算。

Result: ChildRiskGuard在儿童不当短视频检测任务上取得F1分数0.769，显著优于直接应用通用内容安全模型，并与强基准模型保持竞争力。该案例完整记录了AI交互过程、被拒绝的备选方案、修正与审计轨迹，为AI赋能的CDS提供了过程与工件层面的双重证据。

Conclusion: AI4CDS为AI使能的计算设计科学研究提供了负责任的框架，核心贡献在于建立了AI扩展搜索与研究者治理相结合的协作范式；ChildRiskGuard则从实践层面证明了AI扩展的、研究者治理的设计过程能够产生和评估新颖的计算设计知识。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is transforming not only what information systems researchers design, but also how design research is conducted. Yet existing literature offers limited guidance for computational design science (CDS) when AI actively participates in problem formulation, resource construction, design search, evaluation, and knowledge abstraction. We develop AI for Computational Design Science (AI4CDS), a five-phase methodological framework in which AI expands problem and design search while researchers retain responsibility for domain grounding, admissibility, verification, and scientific judgment. Collaboration is governed by graduated trust, reversibility, auditability, and differentiated reproducibility. We instantiate AI4CDS through ChildRiskGuard, an interpretable artifact for detecting short-form videos inappropriate for children, while documenting AI interactions, rejected alternatives, corrections, and audit trails. The case translates audience-dependent safety and explanation faithfulness into three technical challenges and develops an artifact that separates generic from child-specific risk, represents distinct developmental-risk mechanisms, and makes concept-level explanations part of the predictive computation. ChildRiskGuard achieves an F1 score of 0.769, substantially outperforming direct application of a general-purpose content-safety model while remaining competitive with strong benchmarks. The primary contribution is AI4CDS as a responsible framework for AI-enabled CDS; ChildRiskGuard provides process and artifact evidence of how AI-expanded, researcher-governed design can generate and evaluate novel computational design knowledge.

</details>


### [240] [Don't Drop Dropout: Optimizing Layer Sparsity for Efficient LLM Training and Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05275)
*Mostafa Elhoushi,Alex Pretko,Nolan Dey,Bin Claire Zhang,Gavia Gray,Gurpreet Gosal,Abdulrahman Mahmoud,Shane Bergsma,Joel Hestness*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究系统性地证明层丢弃（layer dropout）应被用于大规模语言模型（LLM）的预训练中，通过优化层分布、时间调度和优化器超参数，在相同训练FLOPs下可获得更低的损失，并可节省高达25%的训练FLOPs；同时支持后训练优化（早退、中间层跳过、自推测解码），实现最高1.5倍推理加速且精度损失可忽略。研究涵盖2400+次实验，模型规模从271M到8.2B参数，数据集最高达160B tokens。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管层丢弃已被证明在语言和视觉Transformer中能加速训练、提升精度并增强对零样本层剪枝的鲁棒性，但随着模型和数据集规模扩大，层丢弃在LLM预训练配方中被广泛弃用。此前有研究报道dropout可能损害精度，但缺乏全面量化甚至缓解该效应的研究，因此需要系统性地评估并确立层丢弃在SOTA LLM训练中的最佳实践与扩展性分析。

Method: 通过大规模的实证研究确定层丢弃在LLM预训练中的最佳实践，包括：优化层分布（layer distribution）、时间调度（time schedule）以及优化器超参数（optimizer hyperparameters）。实验在Cerebras CS-3系统上进行，涵盖超过2400次训练实验，模型规模从271M到8.2B参数，数据集规模最高达160B tokens，以系统验证结论在大规模训练场景中的可靠性。

Result: 研究发现：在相同训练FLOPs下，采用优化配置的层丢弃可获得更低的损失；在给定训练步数下，LLM可实现更低或相似的验证损失，同时节省高达25%的训练FLOPs。此外，层丢弃支持显著的后训练优化（早退、中间层跳过、自推测解码），实现最高1.5倍推理加速且精度损失可忽略。2400+次实验验证了这些发现在从271M到8.2B参数规模上的可靠性。

Conclusion: 层丢弃应当被重新纳入SOTA LLM的训练配方中，其对大规模训练具有显著效益。通过适当配置，层丢弃不仅不会损害精度，反而能在相同FLOPs下降低损失、节省训练计算量，并为推理阶段带来可观的加速优化空间。研究结论经过大规模实验验证，可可靠地扩展到前沿训练规模。

Abstract: Layer dropout (a.k.a. stochastic depth) has been shown to enable faster training, higher accuracy, and robustness to zero-shot layer pruning in both language and vision transformers. However, as models and datasets have scaled, dropout - particularly layer dropout - has largely disappeared from large language models (LLMs) pre-training recipes. While some prior work has reported that dropout can degrade accuracy, no comprehensive study has quantified, let alone mitigated, this effect. In this study, we show that layer dropout should be used in state-of-the-art LLM training, establishing best practices and scaling analysis for both training and post-training benefits. Concretely, with optimal layer distribution, time schedule, and optimizer hyperparameters, we observe that at the same training FLOPs layer dropout leads to lower loss. For a given number of training steps, LLMs can achieve lower or similar validation loss while saving upto 25% of training FLOPs. Moreover, layer dropout enables significant post-training optimizations, such as early exit, intermediate-layer skipping, and self-speculative decoding, yielding up to 1.5x inference speedup with negligible accuracy loss. Across more than 2400 training experiments, spanning models from 271M to 8.2B parameters and datasets up to 160B tokens, we demonstrate that these findings extend reliably to large-scale training regimes. All pre-training experiments were run on Cerebras CS-3 systems.

</details>


### [241] [Testing Interchangeability in LLM Agent Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05279)
*Jianxin Gao,Tianyi Yu,Linna Deng,Runze Li,Zining Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文检验生产多智能体系统中"智能体可互换"假设，发现换人几乎不影响任务得分，但会显著增加通信开销（每单位进度通信成本上升16%-63%），且这种代价在Hanabi中甚至高于使用新手。团队独立形成后养成的惯例会产生相互干扰，且形成历史越长，互换代价越大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的多智能体系统经常更换智能体，默认假设同一角色的智能体可完全互换。然而智能体在与队友协作中可能积累私有惯例和知识，替换可能导致协调效率下降。作者旨在系统检验这一假设的可靠性，并量化互换带来的实际代价。

Method: 在同一基础模型上独立构建八支团队执行相同任务，每名智能体在十个组建周期内维护私人笔记本。随后在团队间交换角色匹配的智能体，并在保留任务上测量变化。设置安慰剂对照组以分离"换人打扰"与"换人本身"的影响。进行三项消融实验：改变基础模型、解码温度和组建周期长度，观察互换惩罚如何随团队漂移程度变化。

Result: 互换在任务得分上的代价很小，但通信开销显著上升（每单位进度16%-63%）。Hanabi中互换的智能体比新手更"昂贵"，表明旧伙伴习得的惯例产生干扰。Collab-Overcooked中，主导议程设定的智能体被替换时，额外通信主要来自留下的一方。三项消融均显示互换惩罚与"独立组建团队的漂移程度"同步变化：贪婪解码同时降低两者，延长组建历史同时提高两者。

Conclusion: 在这些场景中，智能体在任务结果上的可互换性优于在协调效率上的可互换性；组建历史越长，互换带来的负面影响越大。这反驳了多智能体系统运维中"角色匹配即可互换"的简单假设，提示需将协调层面的团队特异性纳入系统设计考虑。

Abstract: Production multi-agent systems replace agents constantly, on the assumption that an agent filling a role is interchangeable with any other agent that can do the job. We test that assumption. Eight teams per setting are formed independently from one base model on the same tasks, each agent keeping a private notebook across ten formation episodes; we then trade role-matched agents between teams and measure what changes on held-out tasks. Against a placebo that reproduces the disruption of a roster change without changing who occupies the seat, a swap costs little in task score but raises the communication a team spends per unit of progress by 16 to 63 percent, and in Hanabi a swapped agent is more expensive than an inexperienced one, consistent with interference from conventions learned with its former partner. In Collab-Overcooked, when the agent that sets the agenda is replaced, most of the extra communication comes from the agent that stayed. Three ablations, over base models, decoding temperature and formation length, move the swap penalty alongside one other quantity: how far independently formed teams drift apart. Greedy decoding lowers both; doubling a team's history raises both. In these settings, agents are more fungible in task outcome than in coordination efficiency, with larger swap effects after longer formation histories.

</details>


### [242] [GUT: Quantifying and Optimizing the Reasoning Uncertainty of LLMs via Graph Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05284)
*Shuang Liang,Xin-Yu Hu,Xiang-Jun Ou,Shao-Qun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GUT方法，通过有向无环图建模LLM推理分支，用图复杂度量化推理不确定性（GUT-Q），并通过强化学习以负不确定性作为奖励进行优化（GUT-O），在四个LLM和五个数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的推理过程常表现出不确定性，即使输入相同的提示词，每一步也会产生大量分歧分支，其中部分分支甚至产生明显不可信甚至无意义的推理链和结果。现有的研究缺乏对LLM推理不确定性进行系统量化和优化的有效方法。

Method: 提出了基于图复杂度的不确定性方法（GUT）。核心思想是用有向无环图（DAG）表征每条推理链的潜在分支，确保所有潜在分支在图空间内被全面覆盖。GUT包含两个模块：GUT-Q（量化模块）通过图复杂度近似推理空间复杂度来度量LLM推理不确定性；GUT-O（优化模块）通过将负不确定性作为强化学习中的奖励函数来实现不确定性优化。

Result: 在四个LLM和五个数据集上的实验结果验证了GUT方法的有效性。

Conclusion: GUT方法能够有效量化和降低LLM的推理不确定性，为改善LLM推理可靠性和可信度提供了一种新的理论框架和实用工具。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed great advances in the reasoning ability of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, the reasoning processes of LLMs often exhibit uncertainty, where LLMs often produce a proliferation of divergent branches at each reasoning step even when fed the same prompting inputs, and certain branches exhibit evidently incredible, even nonsensical, reasoning chains and results. In this paper, we propose the Graph-complexity-based UncerTainty (GUT) method for investigating the reasoning uncertainty of LLMs. The key idea of GUT is to characterize the potential branches of each reasoning chain with a directed acyclic graph, thereby ensuring that all potential branches are comprehensively covered within the graph space. Building upon this recognition, we further build two modules of GUT, that is, a Quantification (GUT-Q) module and an Optimization (GUT-O) module, for quantifying and reducing the reasoning uncertainty of LLMs, respectively. GUT-Q measures LLM reasoning uncertainty by approximating the reasoning space complexity with graph complexity. GUT-O implements uncertainty optimization by treating negative uncertainty as the reward function in reinforcement learning. Experimental results conducted on four LLMs and five datasets validate the effectiveness of GUT.

</details>


### [243] [Beyond Aggregate Scores: Behavioral Correctness Assumptions for Assessing Reference-Based Automatic Evaluation Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05289)
*Maria Mahbub,Ashley Rice,Michael R. Munroe,Amidu Kamara,Amir Sadovnik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对基于参考的自动评估方法的补充性元评估框架，通过"行为正确性假设"在受控条件下分析评估器的行为，而非仅依赖与人类判断的一致性。实验发现没有一种评估器能满足所有假设，且聚合性能相似的评估器可能在行为特征上差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的元评估主要衡量与人类判断或基准标签的一致性，这无法在受控条件下深入揭示评估器的行为逻辑。作者希望引入一种互补框架，通过行为层面的诊断来补充传统聚合元评估的局限。

Method: 定义了保持正确性（correctness-preserving）和改变正确性（correctness-altering）假设的分类体系，并通过受控的响应变换来操作化这些假设，规定预期评分行为。对多种词法、字符级、语义、基于LLM及混合评估器进行了系统评估，分析了其在假设层面的行为、稳定性、敏感性、重复运行变异性、配置敏感性及可复现性。

Result: 实验揭示了不同评估范式之间存在明显的行为权衡：没有任何评估器能满足所有提出的正确性假设，且类似聚合性能的评估器可能展现出截然不同的行为特征。这表明行为正确性假设能提供传统聚合元评估所掩盖的诊断信息。

Conclusion: 行为正确性假设作为元评估框架的补充维度，能够提供传统聚合指标无法揭示的诊断性信息，有助于更全面地理解和选择适合具体任务场景的自动评估方法。

Abstract: Automated reference-based evaluation methods play a critical role in assessing natural language generation systems. Existing meta-evaluation primarily measures agreement with human judgments or benchmark labels, providing limited insight into evaluator behavior under controlled conditions. We introduce behavioral correctness assumptions, a complementary framework for evaluating reference-based automatic evaluation methods. We define a taxonomy of correctness-preserving and correctness-altering assumptions and operationalize them through controlled response transformations that specify expected scoring behaviors. We evaluate diverse lexical, character-level, semantic, LLM-based, and hybrid evaluators and analyze their assumption-level behavior, stability, sensitivity, repeat-run variability, configuration sensitivity, and reproducibility. Our experiments reveal distinct behavioral trade-offs across evaluation paradigms: no evaluator satisfies all proposed correctness assumptions, and evaluators with similar aggregate performance can exhibit substantially different behavioral profiles. These findings demonstrate that behavioral correctness assumptions provide diagnostic information obscured by conventional aggregate meta-evaluation.

</details>


### [244] [RISE: Recursive Improvement via Self-Extrapolating Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05295)
*Yang Li,Semih Yavuz,Shafiq Joty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RISE框架，通过从模型自身的RLVR训练轨迹中构造合成教师（基于当前检查点与滞后锚点在参数或logit空间的位移外推），生成稠密token级监督信号，并采用每轮迭代刷新教师的递归蒸馏方式，实现无需外部模型或特权条件的on-policy蒸馏，在数学推理、STEM、代码生成及多轮智能体任务上全面超越RLVR-only和自蒸馏基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy蒸馏（OPD）可为语言模型后训练提供稠密逐token监督，但其效果受限于教师质量：外部教师存在分布不匹配问题，而带有特权条件信息的自蒸馏又受限于上下文学习能力。因此需要一种无需外部模型或特权条件即可获得高质量、与当前模型分布对齐的稠密监督信号的方法。

Method: RISE通过以下步骤实现：1) 从模型自身的RLVR训练轨迹出发，将当前检查点与一个滞后锚点（trailing anchor）之间的位移在参数空间或logit输出空间进行外推，从而构造出一个合成教师；2) 将稀疏的、由结果奖励驱动的参数更新转化为稠密的逐token级监督目标；3) 将RLVR与OPD融合为互补循环：结果奖励保证外推方向朝向正确推理，外推教师精化token级决策；4) 每轮迭代教师随学生进步而刷新，使蒸馏成为递归改进机制而非一次性压缩步骤。

Result: 在数学推理、多领域STEM、代码生成和多轮智能体任务上的实验表明，RISE在所有设置下均优于仅使用RLVR训练和on-policy自蒸馏两种基线方法。

Conclusion: RISE通过从自身训练轨迹中外推测出合成教师，有效解决了外部教师分布失配和特权条件自蒸馏能力受限两大瓶颈，将蒸馏从一阶段压缩转变为迭代式的自改进闭环，为语言模型后训练提供了一种更优的稠密监督方案。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense, per-token supervision for language model post-training, but its effectiveness is bottlenecked by teacher quality: external teachers suffer from distribution mismatch, while self-distillation with privileged conditioning is limited by in-context learning capacity. We propose \textbf{RISE} (\textbf{R}ecursive \textbf{I}mprovement via \textbf{S}elf-\textbf{E}xtrapolating Policy Distillation), which constructs a synthetic teacher directly from the model's own RLVR training trajectory. By extrapolating the displacement between the current checkpoint and a trailing anchor---in parameter space or output logit space---RISE converts a sparse outcome-induced parameter update into a dense token-level target, without any external model or privileged conditioning. RISE combines RLVR and OPD in a complementary loop: outcome rewards ground the extrapolation toward correct reasoning, while the extrapolated teacher refines token-level decisions. Moreover, since the teacher is refreshed every iteration as the student improves, distillation becomes a recursive improvement mechanism rather than a one-shot compression step. Experiments spanning mathematical reasoning, multi-domain STEM, code generation, and multi-turn agentic tasks show that RISE outperforms RLVR-only training and on-policy self-distillation across all settings.

</details>


### [245] [LLM-Driven Algorithm Design for Quantum Circuit Synthesis based on Binary Decision Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05327)
*Yoonju Sim,Federico Berto,Chuanbo Hua,Jinkyoo Park,Changhyun Kwon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出QuantumEvo，一个基于LLM作为启发式生成器的进化框架，用于生成QCC感知的BDD变量排序启发式。发现的HGA-QE启发式修改了遗传算法中的sifting步骤以更好地对齐量子电路成本，在基准测试中实现了70.9%的平局或胜率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电路中的布尔函数需要以可逆方式实现，可逆电路综合通过BDD提供可扩展方案，但结果严重依赖变量排序。现有启发式通常最小化BDD大小作为电路大小的代理，但BDD大小并不能完美反映量子电路成本（QCC），因此需要QCC感知的变量排序方法。

Method: 提出QuantumEvo进化框架，使用LLM作为启发式生成器（而非直接预测排序），从多个启发式家族初始化搜索空间，搜索直接操作用标准BDD操作的变量排序启发式，并通过下游QCC作为选择标准来评估候选启发式。发现的启发式HGA-QE修改了遗传算法中的sifting步骤，使其与QCC更好对齐。

Result: 在基准测试集上，HGA-QE相对于每个函数的最佳基线实现了70.9%的平局或胜率，在13.5%的函数上严格最优。在来自与启发式发现不同数据来源的两个基准套件上，HGA-QE在严格胜率方面表现出更明显的相对优势。

Conclusion: HGA-QE展示了广泛竞争性的QCC性能，特别是在来自不同数据源的基准套件上表现出更好的泛化优势，证明了基于LLM的进化启发式搜索方法在可逆电路综合中用于QCC感知变量排序的有效性。

Abstract: Quantum circuits are central to implementing quantum algorithms on quantum devices, where quantum gates must be reversible. Many quantum algorithms rely on Boolean functions, which must therefore be implemented reversibly within quantum circuits. Reversible circuit synthesis provides a way to translate such Boolean functions into reversible circuits. Binary decision diagrams (BDDs) offer a scalable approach to this task, but the resulting BDDs and circuits depend heavily on variable ordering. Existing ordering heuristics commonly minimize BDD size because it is closely tied to the circuit size. However, BDD size is an imperfect proxy for the quantum cost of the synthesized circuit (QCC). We propose \texttt{QuantumEvo}, an evolutionary framework that uses an LLM as a heuristic generator for QCC-aware BDD variable ordering. Instead of predicting orderings directly, \texttt{QuantumEvo} searches over ordering heuristics initialized from multiple heuristic families. Candidate heuristics directly manipulate variable orderings using standard BDD operations and are selected by downstream QCC. The discovered heuristic, HGA-QE, modifies the sifting step inside a genetic algorithm so that the procedure is better aligned with QCC. Across the benchmark set, HGA-QE achieves a 70.9\% tie-or-win rate against the per-function best baseline and is strictly best on 13.5\% of the functions. The results demonstrate broadly competitive QCC performance, with HGA-QE showing a clearer relative advantage in strict wins on the two benchmark suites drawn from sources different from the data used for heuristic discovery.

</details>


### [246] [Who Should Grade My Work? Student Perspectives on Transparent AI-Assisted Writing Assessment in Higher Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05346)
*Rayed AlGhamdi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究考察了当学生明确知晓评分和反馈由ChatGPT（而非人类教师）生成时，他们如何理解和解读AI中介的学业评价。通过对沙特某公立大学技术传播课程中13名男性计算机专业本科生的写作反思进行归纳主题分析，识别出四个主题：反馈的有用性、AI情境与教学局限的认知、条件性信任（区分反馈效用与评价权威）、以及对人类教师制度与教学角色的反思。核心发现是学生将GenAI反馈视为表面修改的有用工具，但始终认为人类教师才是评分决策的适当权威。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究关注GenAI生成反馈是否提升写作表现及学生对其有用性的感知，但对学生在明确被告知AI系统（而非人类教师）产出评分和反馈时如何解读此类评价缺乏了解。本研究填补该空白，探讨AI评价权威性在高等教育评估中的边界。

Method: 采用定性教学探究法，在沙特某公立大学计算机专业本科技术传播课程中实施。13名男性本科生完成课内手写写作任务，扫描答卷由ChatGPT依据与任务目标对齐的rubric提示进行评分。随后明确告知学生评分与反馈出自ChatGPT，并邀请其书面反思此评价。对反思材料进行归纳主题分析。

Result: 归纳主题分析识别出四个主题：（1）对反馈有用性的感知；（2）对AI情境与教学局限性的意识；（3）条件性信任——将反馈效用与评价权威加以区分；（4）对人类教师在制度与教学层面角色的反思。参与者接受GenAI反馈对表面层面修改有用，但始终将人类教师定位为评分决策的适当权威。

Conclusion: 研究界定了"反馈效用"与"评价权威"之间的区分——学生将这两种判断视为分析上分离的概念，而非同一种满意度量尺的两端。这一发现对AI在高等教育评估中的角色定位及教学实践设计具有启示意义。

Abstract: The integration of GenAI tools into higher education assessment raises important questions about how students understand, interpret, and respond to AI-mediated evaluation. As instructors increasingly explore AI tools for providing feedback, prior research has examined whether GenAI-generated feedback improves writing performance and how students perceive its usefulness; comparatively little is known, however, about how students interpret such evaluation when they are explicitly informed that an AI system, rather than a human instructor, produced the feedback and the score. This study reports findings from a qualitative pedagogical inquiry conducted in an undergraduate technical communication course for computing students at a Saudi public university. Thirteen male undergraduate computing students completed an in-class handwritten writing task; the scanned submissions were evaluated by ChatGPT using a rubric-based prompt aligned with the task objectives. Students were then explicitly informed that ChatGPT had generated the score and feedback and were invited to reflect on the evaluation in writing. Inductive thematic analysis of these reflections identified four themes: perceived usefulness of feedback; awareness of AI's contextual and pedagogical limitations; conditional trust, distinguishing feedback utility from evaluative authority; and reflection on the institutional and pedagogical role of the human instructor. Participants accepted GenAI feedback as useful for surface-level revision but consistently positioned the human instructor as the appropriate authority over grading decisions. The study identifies this as a distinction between feedback utility and evaluative authority, two judgments that students treat as analytically separate rather than as opposite ends of a single approval scale...

</details>


### [247] [CUA-Universe: A Scalable and Dynamic Environment for Hybrid GUI+CLI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05374)
*Haoting Shi,Wenhao Wang,Weicheng Fang,Yaozhong Liang,Tian Jin,Pengxiang Zhao,Guangyi Liu,Siheng Chen,Yanfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CUA-Universe，一个规模化的环境到数据管道，将真实桌面软件转化为混合GUI+CLI环境，并生成数据以训练智能体实现高效的GUI+CLI双重模式协调。训练后的9B模型在多个基准上同时提升了成功率（如OSWorld SR +16.8 pts）并大幅降低步数和token开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机使用智能体主要依赖GUI操作，产生低效轨迹，而真实计算机工作通常需要同时协调视觉状态检查（GUI）与精确高效的命令行操作（CLI）。然而，支持双模式（GUI+CLI）的可扩展混合环境十分稀缺，因为针对每个真实应用构建此类环境需要大量手动工程。此外，现有CLI原生智能体缺乏视觉感知能力，而GUI原生智能体在适合命令行的操作上效率低下。

Method: 提出了CUA-Universe管道，包含三个关键组件：1) App-Forge，将真实桌面应用适配为可复现的虚拟机并发现、包装或生成命令行接口，扩展到16个应用；2) Task-Weave，从可复用的种子文件操作中合成难度可控的多样化混合任务；3) Path-Steer，引导轨迹沿高效混合路径推进并收集经过验证的轨迹用于后训练。

Result: 基于此数据训练的9B模型在多个基准上表现优异：CUA-Verse评分提升+39.3 pts，步数减少37%，token减少60%；OSWorld成功率提升+16.8 pts，步数减少57%，token减少44%；OSWorld-MCP评分提升+7.84 pts，步数减少27%，token减少30%。训练使得智能体行为从低效的GUI交互和脆弱的CLI脚本转向有效的GUI+CLI协同编排。

Conclusion: CUA-Universe提供了一条可扩展的路径，通过将真实软件转化为混合GUI+CLI环境并自动生成训练数据，推动了更强大、更高效的计算机使用智能体的发展。

Abstract: Computer-use agents have advanced on benchmarks like OSWorld and AndroidWorld, but still act mostly through the GUI, often producing inefficient trajectories. Real-world computer work is hybrid, combining visual-state inspection with precise, high-throughput command-line operations, so capable agents must coordinate both modalities over shared application state. Yet scalable hybrid environments remain scarce because supporting both GUI and CLI over real applications typically requires substantial manual engineering for each application. Existing agents also struggle to use the two interfaces complementarily: CLI-native agents lack visual perception for tasks involving interface state or layout, while GUI-native agents are inefficient for operations better executed through commands. We introduce CUA-Universe, a scalable environment-to-data pipeline that turns real desktop software into hybrid GUI+CLI environments. App-Forge adapts applications into reproducible VMs and command-line surfaces it discovers, wraps, or generates, scaling to 16 applications; Task-Weave synthesizes diverse hybrid tasks of controllable difficulty from reusable operations over seed files; and Path-Steer steers rollouts along efficient hybrid paths and harvests verified trajectories for post-training. Training on this data shifts behavior from inefficient GUI interaction and brittle CLI scripting toward effective GUI+CLI orchestration. Our 9B model improves both success and efficiency on CUA-Verse (Score +39.3 pts; -37% steps, -60% tokens), OSWorld (SR +16.8 pts; -57% steps, -44% tokens), and OSWorld-MCP (Score +7.84 pts; -27% steps, -30% tokens). CUA-Universe provides a scalable path toward more capable and efficient computer-use agents.

</details>


### [248] [Molecular Déjà Vu: Digit-Level Retrieval of Published Values in Frontier Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05381)
*Matthias Busch,Marius Tacke,Sviatlana V. Lamaka,Mikhail L. Zheludkevich,Christian J. Cyron,Roland C. Aydin,Christian Feiler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计了22个前沿LLM在12个分子性质回归基准上的逐字检索（verbatim retrieval）现象，发现该现象广泛存在但具有基准特异性；推理水平会影响检索程度（高推理水平下被标记率比最低水平高89%）；通过变换SMILES字符串等方式可部分中断检索，且抑制检索后不同模型的预测误差在相对意义上更趋一致，表明LLM的预测能力不完全由记忆值数量决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在分子性质基准上日益被用于评估，但准确率无法区分模型是真正预测了性质还是检索了已发表数值。这种逐字检索会严重高估模型的实际预测能力，因此需要对检索现象进行系统性审计，以理解模型能力的真实本质。

Method: 对22个前沿模型在12个回归基准上进行逐字检索审计；在两个推理水平下运行实验以检验推理对检索的影响；通过变换SMILES字符串并保留原始标签的对抗性方式来测试中断检索的可能性；比较抑制检索前后不同模型的预测误差差异。

Result: 逐字检索广泛存在但相对基准特异：5个数据集上超过50%的LLM表现出逐字检索，其余数据集上仅在孤立样本中出现；高推理水平下被标记为检索的频率比最低水平高89%；最强模型在部分情况下仍能识别"变换SMILES+原始标签"的组合；抑制检索使不同模型间预测误差在相对意义上更接近，而逐字检索的使用差异则使其分散。

Conclusion: LLM在分子回归基准上的逐字检索现象普遍存在且深度不一，其影响了模型间预测误差的分布格局。研究结果表明，LLM的通用预测能力并非仅由记忆数值的量决定，这为理解LLM在科学基准上的评估有效性提供了重要视角。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly evaluated on molecular property benchmarks, but accuracy cannot distinguish a model that predicts a property from one that retrieves a published number. We audit 22 frontier models on 12 regression benchmarks for verbatim retrieval and find that it is widespread but relatively benchmark-specific: on five datasets more than $50\%$ of the LLMs show verbatim retrieval, while on the remaining datasets it appears only in isolated cells. We run our experiments at two reasoning levels and find that reasoning changes retrieval. The same experiments, on the same molecules and with the same prompt, are flagged $89\%$ more often at the higher reasoning level than at the lowest one. Finally, we test a way to interrupt retrieval in our most contaminated cases, and find that the strongest models in some cases still recognise a combination of transformed SMILES strings and original labels. Furthermore, suppressing retrieval moves the prediction errors of the different models closer together in relative terms, while their differing use of verbatim retrieval spreads them apart. This indicates that the general predictive capability of an LLM is not determined solely by the amount of memorised values. This work provides an overview of the amount and depth of verbatim retrieval in molecular regression benchmarks using LLMs.

</details>


### [249] [Necessary or Sufficient? Evaluating LLM Explanations With Behavioural Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05385)
*Urja Pawar,Rajitha Ramanayake,Nabeel Kemal,Ashwin Kandath,Owen O'Neill,Guillaume Bourgeon,Houssem Chatbri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究者通过黑盒可控干预方法，在两种合成用例中评估了LLM解释中命名因素的必要性与充分性，发现跨八个模型（Claude、GPT、Gemini家族）中，引用排名与必要性和充分性得分的Spearman相关性较弱（0.349-0.580），且不可引用因素经常比最低分引用因素得分更高，表明解释虽包含有用信息但未能可靠识别实际影响最强的因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在智能体工作流中常输出行动相关建议或判断并附带解释，操作者依赖这些命名因素进行监控、诊断和升级决策。这种使用方式假设解释与组件的可观察决策行为一致，但该假设的可靠性此前未经系统验证。

Method: 定义两种解释因素解释：必要性（改变该因素会改变输出）和充分性（保留该因素同时移除其他可变更信息时输出不变）。在两种合成用例（向客户推荐顾问、判断提示词有害性或风险）中，模型输出结果和影响最大的三个因素。通过可控黑盒干预估计每个因素的必需性得分（改变该因素时输出改变频率）和充分性得分（保留该因素时输出保持不变的频率）。评估了Claude、GPT、Gemini家族共八个模型。

Result: 引用排名与必要性/充分性得分的平均Spearman相关性在顾问推荐中为0.349和0.354，在提示词监控中为0.431和0.580。不可引用因素在57.6%（顾问推荐/必要性）、58.1%（顾问推荐/充分性）、25.8%（提示词监控/必要性）和8.9%（提示词监控/充分性）的情况下得分高于引用的最低分因素。引用因素包含有用信息，但未能可靠识别必需性/充分性下实际影响力最强的三个因素。

Conclusion: 该框架为智能体监督中的解释提供了一种黑盒可靠性检查方法，但结果显示影响范围限于单个LLM决策，当前LLM的解释在必要性和充分性两个维度上均无法可靠对齐实际决策行为，需要进一步改进解释的忠实性。

Abstract: LLM decision components that can operate within agent workflows often produce action-relevant recommendations or judgements together with explanations. Operators may use the named factors to monitor a system, diagnose errors, or decide when to escalate an output. Such use assumes that the explanations agree with the component's observable decision behaviour. We test two interpretations of the named factors: necessity, meaning that changing a factor would change the output, and sufficiency, meaning that retaining it while removing other changeable information would preserve the output. We evaluate these interpretations in two synthetic use cases: recommending advisors to clients and judging prompts for harmfulness or risk. Models return an output and the top three factors that most influenced it. Controlled black-box interventions estimate a necessity score for each factor by measuring how often changing it changes the output, and a sufficiency score by measuring how often retaining it preserves the output. Across eight models from the Claude, GPT, and Gemini families, the mean Spearman correlations between the cited ranking and the necessity and sufficiency scores are 0.349 and 0.354 for advisor recommendation, and 0.431 and 0.580 for prompt monitoring. Furthermore, an uncited factor scores above the lowest-scoring cited factor in 57.6% of advisor responses under necessity and 58.1% under sufficiency; the corresponding prompt-monitoring rates are 25.8% and 8.9%. The cited top three contain useful information but do not reliably identify the three factors with the strongest measured influence under necessity or sufficiency. The framework provides a black-box reliability check for explanations used in agent oversight while remaining scoped to individual LLM decisions.

</details>


### [250] [A Deep Generative Model for Synthesizing Labeled Wireless Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05396)
*Yuxiao Li,Keke Hu,Santiago Mazuelas,Yuan Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于深度学习的无线信号生成方法IIns-GAN（Inter-Instance Generative Adversarial Networks），用于生成带有位置标签的真实感无线信号，以解决无线感知中真实数据集采集和标注成本高、传统合成方法依赖环境模型且超参数调优困难的问题。在公开UWB数据集上的实验表明，生成的信号能反映真实测量的物理特性，并有效提升距离估计和环境识别等无线感知任务的模型训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线感知领域需要大量带位置标签的无线信号用于模型训练和性能评估，但真实数据集的采集和标注成本高昂；传统合成方法依赖环境模型，需要大量超参数调优且生成信号的真实性不足，难以满足全面模型训练的需求。

Method: 提出IIns-GAN，一种基于深度学习（GAN框架）的无线信号生成方法，通过捕获样本间关系（inter-instance）来生成与实际环境适配且带标签的真实感无线信号，适用于距离估计和环境识别等多种模型训练任务。

Result: 在公开的超宽带（UWB）数据集上进行了广泛实验，结果表明IIns-GAN生成的信号能够真实反映真实世界测量的物理特性，并对多种无线感知任务中的模型训练带来显著提升。

Conclusion: IIns-GAN是一种有效的解决方案，能够生成兼顾真实性和标签有效性的无线信号，克服了传统合成方法的局限性，为无线感知模型的训练提供了高质量的标注数据来源。

Abstract: Wireless signals with position-related labels are pivotal for both performance evaluation and model training in the realm of wireless sensing. However, acquiring real-world datasets is often challenged by significant measurement and labeling costs. Traditional methods for synthesizing labeled wireless signals typically rely on environmental models, leading to extensive hyper-parameter tuning and inadequate realism for comprehensive model training purposes. To address these limitations, we introduce a novel deep learning (DL)-based method, namely Inter-Instance Generative Adversarial Networks (IIns-GAN), to generate realistic labeled wireless signals. The generated signals are particularly adaptive to different environment scenarios and well-suited for various model training tasks, including distance estimation and environment identification. We have conducted extensive experiments on public Ultra-Wideband (UWB) datasets to evaluate the realism and utility of the generated signals. The results demonstrate that the signals generated by IIns-GAN mirror the physical characteristics of real-world measurements, and significantly contribute to the improvement of model training in diverse wireless sensing tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

### [251] [STyMo: Fast and Controllable Few-Shot Motion Style Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04500)
*Jose Luis Ponton,Alexander Winkler,Ladislav Kavan,Yuting Ye,Petr Kadlecek*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: STyMo是一种少样本动作风格迁移方法，仅需数秒配对数据和1-2分钟训练时间，通过将风格分解为静态姿态成分和时域动态成分，实现可解释、可调节且鲁棒的动作风格化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作风格化方法要么依赖大规模风格化数据集，要么依赖无法泛化到训练分布之外的预训练模型，难以在少量数据条件下支持多样化的动作风格。

Method: 将风格分解为两个组成部分：(1) 静态成分——捕获时间不变的姿态特征；(2) 时域成分——捕获逐帧的动态特征。该分解支持运行时调整姿态强度、时域夸张程度和身体各区域风格。此外引入"可风格化门控"(stylizability gate)来自动防止对分布外动作产生伪影，并支持迭代式创作工作流。

Result: 在多种动作风格上展示了结果，涵盖细微的情感变化到夸张的角色原型。训练只需数秒配对数据和1-2分钟，支持运行时调节且对任意输入具有鲁棒性。发布了处理后的配对数据集以促进后续研究。

Conclusion: STyMo通过风格分解策略显著降低了动作风格化对数据量和计算资源的需求，同时提高了系统的可解释性、可控性和鲁棒性，为多样虚拟角色的风格化提供了实用且高效的few-shot解决方案。

Abstract: Supporting a wide variety of motion styles is critical for creating diverse virtual characters, but current methods either require large stylized datasets or pre-trained models that cannot generalize beyond their training distribution. We present STyMo, a few-shot approach that learns motion style from only seconds of paired data and trains in one to two minutes. Our key insight is to decompose style into two components: a static component capturing time-invariant posture, and a temporal component capturing frame-wise dynamics. This decomposition yields an interpretable system where posture intensity, temporal exaggeration, and per-body-region style can be adjusted at runtime. Furthermore, the reduction in required training data and computation time structurally permits an iterative authoring workflow. To ensure robustness on arbitrary inputs, we further introduce a stylizability gate that automatically prevents artifacts on out-of-distribution motions. We demonstrate results across diverse motion styles, from subtle emotional variations to exaggerated character archetypes, and release our processed paired dataset to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [252] [LayoutShop: Content-Constrained Exploratory Design of Creative Article Layout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05098)
*Jialuo Li,Pengfei Xu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: LayoutShop是一种新颖的计算框架，用于设计能够框定给定文章版面的创意布局。受实际文章版式设计流程启发，允许用户创建或选择版式模板进行概念化，构建布局设计空间以提取合格布局结构，通过优化算法确定几何参数，并用两个神经网络评估布局质量，最终将高质量结果返回用户选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统文章版面设计需要大量人工投入和专业技能，缺乏自动化工具来帮助用户高效创建高质量且富有创意的文章布局，因此需要一种能够结合用户意图与计算优化方法的框架。

Method: 1) 用户创建或选择版式模板进行概念化；2) 利用模板构建布局设计空间以提取合格的布局结构；3) 通过优化算法确定提取的布局结构的几何参数，以框定给定文章；4) 采用两个神经网络对布局进行质量评估；5) 将高质量输出返回给用户进行筛选。

Result: 通过用户研究评估了框架的可用性及所产生文章布局的质量，结果表明该框架能有效帮助用户创建高质量的文章布局。

Conclusion: LayoutShop框架验证了将用户驱动的模板概念化与计算优化及神经网络评估相结合的可行性与有效性，能够切实帮助用户提升文章版面设计的质量与效率。

Abstract: We present LayoutShop, a novel computational framework for designing creative layouts that frame a given article. Inspired by the actual article layout design process, we enable users to create or select layout templates for conceptualization. These templates help construct a layout design space to extract eligible layout structures. Our algorithm then determines the geometry of the extracted layout structures to frame the given article via an optimization approach. We then employ two neural networks for layout assessment, and the high-quality outputs are returned to users for selection. We conducted a user study to evaluate the framework's usability and the quality of the article layouts it produces. The results of the user study confirmed that our framework can effectively help users create high-quality article layouts.

</details>


### [253] [GradRig: Differentiable Weights for Skinned Gaussian Splat Deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05127)
*Nina Vesseron,Élie Michel*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向3D高斯泼溅的无网格蒙皮变形流水线，利用蒙皮权重的空间梯度来延展高斯椭球，避免拉伸时产生空洞，并支持实时WebGL渲染和可选的自适应重采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒙皮变形是常见的3D形状驱动方法，对传统网格只需位移顶点即可变形多边形。但3D高斯泼溅缺乏顶点连接信息，刚性变换点无法在拉伸时防止空洞产生，需要额外的变形策略。

Method: 利用蒙皮权重的空间梯度信息，为高斯泼溅提供完全无网格的变形流程，更准确地延展高斯基元。权重梯度在用户创建骨架结构（rig）时即被评估，并附加一个可选的自适应重采样方案，用于细分仍产生伪影的高斯泼溅。

Result: 该方法能更精确地延展高斯泼溅以避免拉伸空洞，同时保持与实时渲染能力完全兼容，并在WebGL查看器中得到验证。

Conclusion: 该方法通过权重空间梯度实现了高斯泼溅的高质量无网格蒙皮变形，兼顾了渲染质量与实时性能，并为极端拉伸情况提供了可扩展的自适应重采样方案。

Abstract: Skinned deformation is a common framework to turn a 3D shape from its rest pose into a dynamic pose through the deformation of a coarser kinematic structure, called rig. When applied to a 3D mesh, this rig only needs to displace vertices to deform the polygons that connect them. However, when deforming 3D Gaussian Splats, which do not provide connectivity information, rigidly transforming points is not enough to prevent the creation of holes when stretching shapes. In this paper, we use the spatial gradient of skinning weights to provide a full mesh-free deformation pipeline for Gaussian Splats, that more accurately stretches splats while remaining fully compatible with real-time rendering capabilities, which we demonstrate in a WebGL viewer. We present how we evaluate these gradients when the user creates the rig structure and propose an optional adaptive resampling scheme to split up splats that still produce artifacts.

</details>
